# Example output of Proteome Discoverer converter

output using example data provided in package

## Usage

``` r
pd_testing_output
```

## Format

a list with 2 data.frames

## Details

The experiment did not contain a global profiling run, but we show an
example of extracting the unmodified peptides and using them in place of
the profiling run.

## Examples

``` r
head(pd_testing_output)
```
